  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Reverses an emoji-encoded text string back into the original UTF-8 text.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If emojiData is null, reject.
   *   2. If emojiData is empty, return an empty string.
   *   3. For each Unicode code point (emoji symbol) in emojiData, look it up in reverseMap; reject if the symbol is absent.
   *   4. Convert each reverse-mapped chunk index to bitsPerChunk bits, MSB first, and concatenate them.
   *   5. Convert the concatenated bit stream to bytes, padding the final partial byte with zero bits if needed.
   *   6. Parse the first 4 bytes as a big-endian length prefix.
   *   7. Keep exactly that many processed bytes after the prefix and discard padding bytes.
   *   8. Call unprocessData on those processed bytes and return the resulting bytes as a UTF-8 string.
   * Related Features: reverseMap, bitsPerChunk, unprocessData
   * Related Classes: EMap, EByteArray, CompressionMethod
   * Input: emojiData : String - an emoji string to decode; null and invalid symbols are rejected; empty returns empty.
   * Output: String - the decoded UTF-8 text; empty if emojiData is empty.
   * Pre-condition: EmojiCodec is initialized.
   * Post-condition: If emojiData is null or contains a symbol absent from the active reverseMap, the operation rejects; otherwise, if emojiData was produced by encode, the original text is returned.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Encodes UTF-8 text into an emoji string using the active recipe alphabet.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If data is null, reject.
   *   2. Convert data to raw UTF-8 bytes.
   *   3. If raw bytes are empty, return an empty emoji string.
   *   4. Call processData(raw) to optionally compress the byte stream.
   *   5. Prepend a 4-byte big-endian length prefix equal to the processed byte length.
   *   6. Convert the resulting payload to a bit stream, MSB first.
   *   7. Split the bit stream into bitsPerChunk-sized chunks, padding the final chunk with zero bits if needed.
   *   8. Map each chunk value through emojiMap and append the resulting emoji character.
   * Related Features: processData, emojiMap, bitsPerChunk, mask, baseSize, compression
   * Related Classes: EMap, EByteArray, CompressionMethod
   * Input: data : String - UTF-8 text to encode; must be non-null; empty allowed.
   * Output: String - emoji string using only active recipe symbols; empty for empty input.
   * Pre-condition: EmojiCodec is initialized; recipeType, compression mode, and maps are configured.
   * Post-condition: decode(encode(data)) equals data; empty text remains an empty emoji string.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String encode(final String data)
  {
    if (data == null)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("data must not be null");
    }
    byte[] raw = data.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    if (raw.length == 0)
    {
      return "";
    }
    byte[] processed = processData(raw);
    ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
    int len = processed.length;
    baos.write((len >>> 24) & 0xFF);
    baos.write((len >>> 16) & 0xFF);
    baos.write((len >>> 8) & 0xFF);
    baos.write(len & 0xFF);
    baos.write(processed, 0, len);
    byte[] payload = baos.toByteArray();
    StringBuilder bits = new StringBuilder(payload.length * 8);
    for (byte b : payload)
    {
      for (int i = 7; i >= 0; i--)
      {
        bits.append((b >> i) & 1);
      }
    }
    int bpc = getBitsPerChunk();
    if (bpc <= 0)
    {
      throw new IllegalStateException("bitsPerChunk must be positive");
    }
    StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();
    int totalBits = bits.length();
    for (int i = 0; i < totalBits; i += bpc)
    {
      int chunkValue = 0;
      for (int j = 0; j < bpc; j++)
      {
        int pos = i + j;
        if (pos < totalBits)
        {
          chunkValue = (chunkValue << 1) | (bits.charAt(pos) - '0');
        }
        else
        {
          chunkValue = (chunkValue << 1);
        }
      }
      String emoji = getEmojiMap().get(chunkValue);
      if (emoji == null)
      {
        throw new IllegalStateException("No emoji mapped for chunk value " + chunkValue);
      }
      result.append(emoji);
    }
    return result.toString();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Reverses an emoji-encoded text string back into the original UTF-8 text.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If emojiData is null, reject.
   *   2. If emojiData is empty, return an empty string.
   *   3. For each Unicode code point (emoji symbol) in emojiData, look it up in reverseMap; reject if the symbol is absent.
   *   4. Convert each reverse-mapped chunk index to bitsPerChunk bits, MSB first, and concatenate them.
   *   5. Convert the concatenated bit stream to bytes, padding the final partial byte with zero bits if needed.
   *   6. Parse the first 4 bytes as a big-endian length prefix.
   *   7. Keep exactly that many processed bytes after the prefix and discard padding bytes.
   *   8. Call unprocessData on those processed bytes and return the resulting bytes as a UTF-8 string.
   * Related Features: reverseMap, bitsPerChunk, unprocessData
   * Related Classes: EMap, EByteArray, CompressionMethod
   * Input: emojiData : String - an emoji string to decode; null and invalid symbols are rejected; empty returns empty.
   * Output: String - the decoded UTF-8 text; empty if emojiData is empty.
   * Pre-condition: EmojiCodec is initialized.
   * Post-condition: If emojiData is null or contains a symbol absent from the active reverseMap, the operation rejects; otherwise, if emojiData was produced by encode, the original text is returned.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String decode(final String emojiData)
  {
    if (emojiData == null)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("emojiData must not be null");
    }
    if (emojiData.isEmpty())
    {
      return "";
    }
    int bpc = getBitsPerChunk();
    if (bpc <= 0)
    {
      throw new IllegalStateException("bitsPerChunk must be positive");
    }
    StringBuilder bits = new StringBuilder();
    int index = 0;
    while (index < emojiData.length())
    {
      int cp = emojiData.codePointAt(index);
      String symbol = new String(Character.toChars(cp));
      Integer chunkValue = getReverseMap().get(symbol);
      if (chunkValue == null)
      {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol not found in reverseMap: " + symbol);
      }
      for (int i = bpc - 1; i >= 0; i--)
      {
        bits.append((chunkValue >> i) & 1);
      }
      index += Character.charCount(cp);
    }
    int bitLen = bits.length();
    int byteLen = (bitLen + 7) / 8;
    byte[] bytes = new byte[byteLen];
    for (int i = 0; i < bitLen; i++)
    {
      if (bits.charAt(i) == '1')
      {
        bytes[i / 8] |= (1 << (7 - (i % 8)));
      }
    }
    if (bytes.length < 4)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid encoded data: too short");
    }
    int len = ((bytes[0] & 0xFF) << 24) | ((bytes[1] & 0xFF) << 16) | ((bytes[2] & 0xFF) << 8) | (bytes[3] & 0xFF);
    if (bytes.length - 4 < len)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid encoded data: length prefix exceeds payload");
    }
    byte[] processed = new byte[len];
    System.arraycopy(bytes, 4, processed, 0, len);
    byte[] original = unprocessData(processed);
    return new String(original, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
  }

   * Summary: Computes encoding statistics for a text encode operation.

  public Map<String, Double> getStats(final String original, final String encoded)
  {
    int originalBytes = original.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length;
    int encodedLength = encoded.codePointCount(0, encoded.length());
    double bitsPerEmoji = getBitsPerChunk();
    double theoreticalRatio = bitsPerEmoji / 8.0;
    double actualRatio = encodedLength > 0 ? (double) originalBytes / encodedLength : 0.0;
    Map<String, Double> stats = new LinkedHashMap<String, Double>();
    stats.put("original_bytes", (double) originalBytes);
    stats.put("encoded_length", (double) encodedLength);
    stats.put("actual_ratio", actualRatio);
    stats.put("theoretical_ratio", theoreticalRatio);
    stats.put("bits_per_emoji", bitsPerEmoji);
    return stats;
  }

  public EList<Object> decodeBinary(final String encoded)

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Performs a file-oriented encode or decode workflow and returns result statistics.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If operation is "encode":
   *      a. Read the input file with fileHandler.readFile(inputPath), obtaining FileData fd.
   *      b. Set originalBytes = fd.content.length.

   *   3. For any other operation value, reject.
   * Related Features: fileHandler, encodeBinary, decodeBinary
   * Related Classes: FileHandler, FileData, EMap, EByteArray, EJavaObject
   * Input: inputPath : String - source file path; outputPath : String - destination file path; operation : String - "encode" or "decode".
   * Output: EMap - file encoding or decoding result.
   * Pre-condition: inputPath is readable; outputPath may be missing parent directories; operation is valid.
   * Post-condition: The output file is written, missing parents are created, and the returned map contains the required fields.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public Map<String, Object> processFile(final String inputPath, final String outputPath, final String operation)
  {
    if (operation == null)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("operation must not be null");
    }
    if ("encode".equals(operation))
    {
      FileData fd = getFileHandler().readFile(inputPath);
      int originalBytes = fd.getContent().length;
      String encodedEmoji = encodeBinary(fd.getMimeType(), fd.getContent());
      int encodedLength = encodedEmoji.codePointCount(0, encodedEmoji.length());
      String mimeType = fd.getMimeType();
      double actualRatio = encodedLength > 0 ? (double) originalBytes / encodedLength : 0.0;
      ensureParentDirectory(outputPath);
      getFileHandler().writeFile(outputPath, encodedEmoji.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
      Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
      result.put("original_bytes", originalBytes);
      result.put("encoded_length", encodedLength);
      result.put("mime_type", mimeType);
      result.put("actual_ratio", actualRatio);
      return result;
    }
    else if ("decode".equals(operation))
    {
      FileData fd = getFileHandler().readFile(inputPath);
      String emojiText = new String(fd.getContent(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      EList<Object> resultList = decodeBinary(emojiText);
      byte[] decodedBytes = (byte[]) resultList.get(0);
      String mimeType = (String) resultList.get(1);
      int decodedSize = decodedBytes.length;
      ensureParentDirectory(outputPath);
      getFileHandler().writeFile(outputPath, decodedBytes);
      Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
      result.put("decoded_size", decodedSize);
      result.put("mime_type", mimeType);
      return result;
    }
    else
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown operation: " + operation);
    }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Returns file information needed to decide whether a file can be processed.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Try to read the file with fileHandler.readFile(filePath).
   *   2. If reading fails, return an EMap with can_process=false and an error message.
   *   3. Otherwise, use the read FileData to obtain size and MIME type.
   *   4. Call suggestRecipe(size) to choose a recipe.
   *   5. Return an EMap containing size, mime_type, can_process=true, and suggested_recipe.
   * Related Features: fileHandler, suggestRecipe
   * Related Classes: FileHandler, FileData, EMap
   * Input: filePath : String - path to the file being inspected.
   * Output: EMap - with size, mime_type, can_process, suggested_recipe; on failure, can_process=false and error.
   * Pre-condition: filePath is non-null.
   * Post-condition: If the file is readable, a suggestion is returned; otherwise the error result clearly marks can_process=false.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public Map<String, Object> getFileInfo(final String filePath)
  {
    try
    {
      FileData fd = getFileHandler().readFile(filePath);
      int size = fd.getContent().length;
      String mimeType = fd.getMimeType();
      String recipe = suggestRecipe(size);
      Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
      result.put("size", size);
      result.put("mime_type", mimeType);
      result.put("can_process", true);
      result.put("suggested_recipe", recipe);
      return result;
    }
    catch (Exception e)
    {
      Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
      result.put("can_process", false);
      result.put("error", e.getMessage());
      return result;
    }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Applies the configured compression mode to a byte array.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If compression equals CompressionMethod.none, return data as a copy.
   *   2. If compression equals CompressionMethod.zlib, compress data using standard zlib compression and return the compressed bytes.
   * Related Features: compression, compressionMethod
   * Related Classes: CompressionMethod, EByteArray
   * Input: data : EByteArray - raw bytes to process.
   * Output: EByteArray - processed bytes; unchanged for none, zlib-compressed for zlib.
   * Pre-condition: data is non-null; compressionMethod is a supported mode.
   * Post-condition: The returned bytes are the reversible processed form expected by unprocessData.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public byte[] processData(final byte[] data)
  {
    String mode = getCompression();
    String none = null;
    String zlib = null;
    if (getCompressionMethod() != null)
    {
      none = getCompressionMethod().getNone();
      zlib = getCompressionMethod().getZlib();
    }
    if (mode == null || mode.equals(none))
    {
      return data.clone();
    }
    if (mode.equals(zlib))
    {
      try
      {
        ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        try (DeflaterOutputStream dos = new DeflaterOutputStream(baos))
        {
          dos.write(data);
        }
        return baos.toByteArray();
      }
      catch (IOException e)
      {
        throw new RuntimeException("Compression failed", e);
      }
    }
    return data.clone();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Reverses the compression applied by processData.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If compression equals CompressionMethod.none, return data as a copy.
   *   2. If compression equals CompressionMethod.zlib, decompress data using standard zlib decompression and return the original bytes.
   * Related Features: compression, compressionMethod
   * Related Classes: CompressionMethod, EByteArray
   * Input: data : EByteArray - processed bytes to reverse.
   * Output: EByteArray - original bytes restored by decompression.
   * Pre-condition: data is non-null; for zlib mode, data must be a valid zlib stream.
   * Post-condition: The returned bytes are the original input to processData.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public byte[] unprocessData(final byte[] data)
  {
    String mode = getCompression();
    String none = null;
    String zlib = null;
    if (getCompressionMethod() != null)
    {
      none = getCompressionMethod().getNone();
      zlib = getCompressionMethod().getZlib();
    }
    if (mode == null || mode.equals(none))
    {
      return data.clone();
    }
    if (mode.equals(zlib))
    {
      try
      {
        ByteArrayInputStream bais = new ByteArrayInputStream(data);
        ByteArrayOutputStream baos = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        try (InflaterInputStream iis = new InflaterInputStream(bais))
        {
          byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
          int n;
          while ((n = iis.read(buffer)) != -1)
          {
            baos.write(buffer, 0, n);
          }
        }
        return baos.toByteArray();
      }
      catch (IOException e)
      {
        throw new RuntimeException("Decompression failed", e);
      }
    }
    return data.clone();
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Produces an integrity digest for a byte array according to the configured verification mode.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If verification equals VerificationMethod.none, return an empty string.
   *   2. Compute the SHA-256 digest over data.
   *   3. Convert the digest bytes to lowercase hexadecimal, left-padding with zeroes so the result has 64 characters.
   * Related Features: verification, verificationMethod
   * Related Classes: VerificationMethod, EByteArray
   * Input: data : EByteArray - the bytes to hash; must be non-null.
   * Output: String - lowercase hexadecimal SHA-256 digest of length 64, or an empty string when verification mode is none.
   * Pre-condition: verification is either "none" or "sha256"; data is non-null.
   * Post-condition: For sha256, a 64-character lowercase hex string is returned; for none, an empty string is returned.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String calculateHash(final byte[] data)
  {
    String mode = getVerification();
    String none = null;
    String sha256 = null;
    if (getVerificationMethod() != null)
    {
      none = getVerificationMethod().getNone();
      sha256 = getVerificationMethod().getSha256();
    }
    if (mode == null || mode.equals(none))
    {
      return "";
    }
    if (mode.equals(sha256))
    {
      try
      {
        MessageDigest md = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
        byte[] digest = md.digest(data);
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (byte b : digest)
        {
          sb.append(String.format("%02x", b));
        }
        return sb.toString();
      }
      catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e)
      {
        throw new RuntimeException("SHA-256 algorithm not available", e);
      }
    }
    return "";
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Prepares binary data for JSON wrapping before emoji encoding.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. content = Base64.encode(data).
   *   2. size = data.length.
   *   3. If mimeType is null, set it to "application/octet-stream".
   *   4. Return a new PreparedData(content, mimeType, size).
   * Related Features: none
   * Related Classes: PreparedData, EByteArray
   * Input: mimeType : String - MIME type of the data; data : EByteArray - raw binary content.
   * Output: PreparedData - contains Base64 content, MIME type, and original byte size.
   * Pre-condition: data is non-null; mimeType may be null and is then defaulted.
   * Post-condition: The PreparedData fields preserve the binary content, MIME type, and original size.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public PreparedData prepareBinaryData(final String mimeType, final byte[] data)
  {
    String content = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(data);
    int size = data.length;
    String mime = mimeType;
    if (mime == null)
    {
      mime = "application/octet-stream";
    }
    PreparedData prepared = FreddyrodgerFactory.eINSTANCE.createPreparedData();
    prepared.setContent(content);
    prepared.setMimeType(mime);
    prepared.setSize(size);
    return prepared;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Restores original binary bytes from a PreparedData object.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Base64-decode preparedData.content.
   *   2. If the decoded length does not equal preparedData.size, reject.
   *   3. Return the decoded byte array.
   * Related Features: none
   * Related Classes: PreparedData, EByteArray
   * Input: preparedData : PreparedData - prepared binary wrapper.
   * Output: EByteArray - original binary bytes.
   * Pre-condition: preparedData is non-null; content is valid Base64; size matches the content length.
   * Post-condition: The returned byte array is the original binary data.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public byte[] restoreBinaryData(final PreparedData preparedData)
  {
    byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(preparedData.getContent());
    if (decoded.length != preparedData.getSize())
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("PreparedData size mismatch: expected " + preparedData.getSize() + " but was " + decoded.length);
    }
    return decoded;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Restores original binary bytes from an EMap representing the JSON binary wrapper.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Read content from the map; it must be a Base64 string.
   *   2. Read size from the map; it must be the expected byte count.
   *   3. Base64-decode content.
   *   4. Verify the decoded length equals size.
   *   5. Return the decoded byte array.
   * Related Features: none
   * Related Classes: EMap, EByteArray
   * Input: data : EMap - map containing content, mime_type, and size fields.
   * Output: EByteArray - original binary bytes.
   * Pre-condition: data is non-null; required fields exist and are well-formed.
   * Post-condition: The returned byte array matches the size field.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public byte[] restoreBinaryDataFromMap(final Map<String, Object> data)
  {
    String content = (String) data.get("content");
    Object sizeObj = data.get("size");
    int size = sizeObj instanceof Number ? ((Number) sizeObj).intValue() : Integer.parseInt(String.valueOf(sizeObj));
    byte[] decoded = Base64.getDecoder().decode(content);
    if (decoded.length != size)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException("Decoded size mismatch: expected " + size + " but was " + decoded.length);
    }
    return decoded;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Selects a recipe name based on file size.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If size < 1024, return "quick".
   *   2. If size < 10240, return "light".
   *   3. If size < 102400, return "classic".
   *   4. Otherwise, return "gourmet".
   * Related Features: none
   * Related Classes: None
   * Input: size : int - file size in bytes; must be non-negative.
   * Output: String - one of "quick", "light", "classic", or "gourmet".
   * Pre-condition: size >= 0.
   * Post-condition: The returned recipe matches the file-size thresholds.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String suggestRecipe(final int size)
  {
    if (size < 1024)
    {
      return "quick";
    }
    if (size < 10240)
    {
      return "light";
    }
    if (size < 102400)
    {
      return "classic";
    }
    return "gourmet";
  }

  /**
   * Escapes a string for safe inclusion in a JSON object literal.
   */
  private String jsonEscape(String s)
  {
    if (s == null)
    {
      return "";
    }
    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
    for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++)
    {
      char c = s.charAt(i);
      switch (c)
      {
        case '\\':
          sb.append("\\\\");
          break;
        case '"':
          sb.append("\\\"");
          break;
        case '\n':
          sb.append("\\n");
          break;
        case '\r':
          sb.append("\\r");
          break;
        case '\t':
          sb.append("\\t");
          break;
        default:
          sb.append(c);
      }
    }
    return sb.toString();
  }

  /**
   * Parses a simple JSON object string into a Map.
   */
  private Map<String, Object> parseJsonObject(String json)
  {
    Map<String, Object> map = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    int i = 1;
    while (i < json.length())
    {
      while (i < json.length() && Character.isWhitespace(json.charAt(i)))
      {
        i++;
      }
      if (i >= json.length())
      {
        break;
      }
      char c = json.charAt(i);
      if (c == '}')
      {
        break;
      }
      if (c == ',')
      {
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      if (c == '"')
      {
        StringBuilder key = new StringBuilder();
        i++;
        while (i < json.length() && json.charAt(i) != '"')
        {
          if (json.charAt(i) == '\\' && i + 1 < json.length())
          {
            i++;
            char e = json.charAt(i);
            switch (e)
            {
              case 'n': key.append('\n'); break;
              case 'r': key.append('\r'); break;
              case 't': key.append('\t'); break;
              case '"': key.append('"'); break;
              case '\\': key.append('\\'); break;
              default: key.append(e);
            }
          }
          else
          {
            key.append(json.charAt(i));
          }
          i++;
        }
        i++;
        while (i < json.length() && Character.isWhitespace(json.charAt(i)))
        {
          i++;
        }
        if (i < json.length() && json.charAt(i) == ':')
        {
          i++;
          while (i < json.length() && Character.isWhitespace(json.charAt(i)))
          {
            i++;
          }
          if (i < json.length() && json.charAt(i) == '"')
          {
            StringBuilder val = new StringBuilder();
            i++;
            while (i < json.length() && json.charAt(i) != '"')
            {
              if (json.charAt(i) == '\\' && i + 1 < json.length())
              {
                i++;
                char e = json.charAt(i);
                switch (e)
                {
                  case 'n': val.append('\n'); break;
                  case 'r': val.append('\r'); break;
                  case 't': val.append('\t'); break;
                  case '"': val.append('"'); break;
                  case '\\': val.append('\\'); break;
                  default: val.append(e);
                }
              }
              else
              {
                val.append(json.charAt(i));
              }
              i++;
            }
            i++;
            map.put(key.toString(), val.toString());
          }
          else
          {
            StringBuilder val = new StringBuilder();
            while (i < json.length() && json.charAt(i) != ',' && json.charAt(i) != '}' && !Character.isWhitespace(json.charAt(i)))
            {
              val.append(json.charAt(i));
              i++;
            }
            String v = val.toString();
            if (v.matches("-?\\d+"))
            {
              map.put(key.toString(), Integer.parseInt(v));
            }
            else
            {
              map.put(key.toString(), v);
            }
          }
        }
      }
      else
      {
        i++;
      }
    }
    return map;
  }

  /**
   * Ensures the parent directory of a file path exists.
   */
  private void ensureParentDirectory(String filePath)
  {
    Path path = Paths.get(filePath);
    Path parent = path.getParent();
    if (parent != null)
    {
      try
      {
        Files.createDirectories(parent);
      }
      catch (IOException e)
      {
        throw new RuntimeException("Unable to create parent directory: " + parent, e);
      }
    }
  }

# Task
You will be given a **Java Class** that is originally generated by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true` and later is completed by LLMs. Your task is to fix the **Compilation Errors** by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->

   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='manual'"
   * @generated
   */
  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='manual'"
   * @generated
   */
  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='manual'"
   * @generated
   */
  public EList<Map<String, Object>> batchProcess(final String filePattern, final String outputDir, final String operation)
  {
      try {
            Path outDir = Path.of(outputDir);
            Files.createDirectories(outDir);
    
            Path patternPath = Path.of(filePattern);
            Path directory = patternPath.getParent();
    
            if (directory == null) {
                directory = Path.of(".");
            }
    
            String glob = patternPath.getFileName().toString();
    
            PathMatcher matcher = directory
                .getFileSystem()
                .getPathMatcher("glob:" + glob);
    
            BasicEList<Path> files = new BasicEList<Path>();
    
            try (DirectoryStream<Path> stream =
                    Files.newDirectoryStream(directory)) {
    
                for (Path entry : stream) {
                    if (Files.isRegularFile(entry)
                            && matcher.matches(entry.getFileName())) {
                        files.add(entry);
                    }
                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

                }
            }
    
            BasicEList<Map<String, Object>> results =
                new BasicEList<Map<String, Object>>();
    
            for (Path inputFile : files) {
                String stem = inputFile.getFileName().toString();
                int dotIndex = stem.lastIndexOf('.');
    
                if (dotIndex > 0) {
                    stem = stem.substring(0, dotIndex);
                }
    
                Path outputPath;
    
                if ("encode".equals(operation)) {
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(stem + ".emoji");
                } else {
                    String mimeType =
                        URLConnection.guessContentTypeFromName(
                            inputFile.getFileName().toString()
                        );
    
                    String extension =
                        mimeType != null ? ".bin" : ".bin";
    
                    outputPath = outDir.resolve(
                        "decoded_" + stem + extension
                    );
                }
    
                try {
                    Map<String, Object> statistics = processFile(
                        inputFile.toString(),
                        outputPath.toString(),
                        operation
                    );
    
                    statistics.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    statistics.put("success", Boolean.TRUE);
    
                    results.add(statistics);
                } catch (Exception exception) {
                    Map<String, Object> errorResult =
                        new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
    
                    errorResult.put("file", inputFile.toString());
                    errorResult.put("success", Boolean.FALSE);
                    errorResult.put("error", exception.getMessage());
    
                    results.add(errorResult);
                }
            }
    
            return results;
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                "Batch processing error: " + exception.getMessage(),
                exception
            );
        }
  }

You will be given a **Java Class** that is originally generated by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true` and later is completed by LLMs. Your task is to fix the **Compilation Errors** by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.
